| `intro` | {<br>  "ko": "집을 보러 가기 전에 월세, 관리비, 난방비와 교통비를 한 표에 놓고 비교합니다.",<br>  "de": "Vor der Besichtigung werden Kaltmiete, Nebenkosten, Heizung und Fahrtkosten in einer Übersicht verglichen.",<br>  "en": "Before viewing a flat, you compare base rent, service charges, heating and commuting costs in one table."<br>} |
| `grammarIds` | [<br>  "grammar_b2_in_light_of"<br>] |
| `dialog` | [<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "광고에는 월세만 나와 있어서 실제 부담을 알기 어렵네요.",<br>    "de": "In der Anzeige steht nur die Kaltmiete, deshalb ist die tatsächliche Belastung schwer einzuschätzen.",<br>    "en": "The listing only shows the base rent, so the real cost is hard to judge."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "관리비와 난방비를 합치면 한 달에 얼마예요?",<br>    "de": "Wie hoch ist die Summe aus Nebenkosten und Heizung pro Monat?",<br>    "en": "How much are the service and heating costs together each month?"<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "최근 명세서에 비추어 볼 때 총주거비는 예산 상한에 가까워요.",<br>    "de": "Im Licht der letzten Abrechnung liegt die gesamte Wohnbelastung nahe an unserer Budgetgrenze.",<br>    "en": "In light of the latest statement, the total housing cost is close to our budget ceiling."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "대신 회사까지 자전거로 갈 수 있어서 교통비는 줄겠어요.",<br>    "de": "Dafür könnten Sie mit dem Rad zur Arbeit fahren und Fahrtkosten sparen.",<br>    "en": "On the other hand, you could cycle to work and save on commuting."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "재택근무가 주 이틀이라 거리보다 난방 상태가 더 중요해요.",<br>    "de": "Da ich zwei Tage pro Woche zu Hause arbeite, ist der Zustand der Heizung wichtiger als die Entfernung.",<br>    "en": "Since I work from home two days a week, the heating matters more than the distance."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "그럼 중개인에게 최근 관리비 명세서와 에너지 정보를 요청해요.",<br>    "de": "Dann bitten Sie den Makler um die letzte Nebenkostenabrechnung und die Energiedaten.",<br>    "en": "Then ask the agent for the latest service-charge statement and energy information."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "좋아요. 추가 비용까지 확인한 뒤에 지원하겠다고 쓸게요.",<br>    "de": "Gut. Ich schreibe, dass ich mich nach Prüfung aller Zusatzkosten bewerbe.",<br>    "en": "Good. I'll write that I'll apply after checking all additional costs."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "총액을 기준으로 보면 싼 광고에 서두를 필요가 없겠네요.",<br>    "de": "Wenn die Gesamtkosten zählen, müssen Sie sich von einer niedrigen Anzeige nicht drängen lassen.",<br>    "en": "If you judge by the total, there is no need to rush because the advertised rent looks low."<br>  }<br>] |

| `intro` | {<br>  "ko": "온라인 채용 설명회에서 자동 선별의 역할과 지원자의 설명 기회를 구체적으로 묻습니다.",<br>  "de": "In einer Online-Infoveranstaltung wird konkret nach der Rolle automatischer Vorauswahl und nach Erklärungsmöglichkeiten für Bewerbende gefragt.",<br>  "en": "At an online recruitment session, you ask exactly how automated screening works and how applicants can explain their case."<br>} |
| `grammarIds` | [<br>  "grammar_b2_not_automatic_conclusion"<br>] |
| `dialog` | [<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "지원서를 먼저 자동으로 선별한다고 들었습니다.",<br>    "de": "Ich habe gehört, dass Bewerbungen zunächst automatisch vorsortiert werden.",<br>    "en": "I heard applications are screened automatically first."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "minsu",<br>    "ko": "네, 필수 조건과 경력 정보를 확인하는 데 활용합니다.",<br>    "de": "Ja, das System prüft Pflichtanforderungen und Angaben zur Berufserfahrung.",<br>    "en": "Yes, the system checks required criteria and work-experience information."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "점수가 낮다고 해서 직무 역량까지 부족한 것은 아니잖아요.",<br>    "de": "Ein niedriger Wert bedeutet doch nicht automatisch, dass auch die berufliche Kompetenz fehlt.",<br>    "en": "A low score does not automatically mean the applicant lacks job skills."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "minsu",<br>    "ko": "맞습니다. 최종 면접 대상은 채용 담당자가 다시 검토합니다.",<br>    "de": "Richtig. | `intro` | {<br>  "ko": "동네 모임에서 주택 부족과 새 이웃의 정착 문제를 한 원인으로 뭉뚱그리지 않고 필요한 지원을 나눠 봅니다.",<br>  "de": "Bei einem Nachbarschaftstreffen werden Wohnungsmangel und Ankommen neuer Nachbarinnen und Nachbarn nicht zu einer einzigen Ursache vermischt.",<br>  "en": "At a neighborhood meeting, you keep housing shortages separate from the support new neighbors need."<br>} |
| `grammarIds` | [<br>  "grammar_b2_reasoned_perspective"<br>] |
| `dialog` | [<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "요즘 집 구하기가 어려워진 원인을 새 이웃에게만 돌리는 말이 나와요.",<br>    "de": "Zurzeit wird die schwierige Wohnungssuche teilweise allein neuen Nachbarn angelastet.",<br>    "en": "Some people are blaming new neighbors alone for how hard it is to find housing."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "주택이 부족한 건 사실이지만 원인은 하나가 아니죠.",<br>    "de": "Wohnungsmangel ist real, hat aber nicht nur eine Ursache.",<br>    "en": "The housing shortage is real, but it does not have just one cause."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "서로 처지가 다르기에 필요한 지원도 다르게 봐야 해요.",<br>    "de": "Da die Lebenslagen verschieden sind, müssen auch die nötigen Hilfen differenziert betrachtet werden.",<br>    "en": "Because people's situations differ, we need to look at support needs differently."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "새로 온 분들은 언어 수업과 행정 안내가 특히 필요하다고 했어요.",<br>    "de": "Neu Zugezogene nannten vor allem Sprachkurse und Orientierung bei Behörden.",<br>    "en": "New arrivals said they especially need language classes and guidance with public services."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "경력이 있어도 자격이 인정되지 않아 일자리를 못 찾는 경우도 있고요.",<br>    "de": "Manche finden trotz Berufserfahrung keine Stelle, weil ihre Qualifikation nicht anerkannt wird.",<br>    "en": "Some cannot find work despite experience because their qualifications are not recognized."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "그럼 주택 공급, 언어 지원, 자격 인정을 별도 안건으로 나눌까요?",<br>    "de": "Sollen wir Wohnungsangebot, Sprachförderung und Anerkennung als getrennte Punkte behandeln?",<br>    "en": "Should we treat housing supply, language support and recognition as separate agenda items?"<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "네. 책임을 한 집단에 묻기보다 해결 경로를 구체적으로 적어요.",<br>    "de": "Ja. Statt einer Gruppe die Schuld zu geben, halten wir konkrete Lösungswege fest.",<br>    "en": "Yes. | `title` | {<br>  "ko": "조회수보다 참여 방식부터",<br>  "de": "Erst die Beteiligung, dann die Klicks",<br>  "en": "Participation before views"<br>} |
| `intro` | {<br>  "ko": "지역 K-컬처 행사에서 짧은 영상 홍보뿐 아니라 처음 온 사람도 참여할 수 있는 프로그램을 설계합니다.",<br>  "de": "Für ein lokales K-Kultur-Festival wird neben Kurzvideo-Werbung ein Programm geplant, an dem auch Neulinge teilnehmen können.",<br>  "en": "For a local K-culture festival, you design more than short-form promotion so newcomers can join in too."<br>} |
| `grammarIds` | [<br>  "grammar_b2_shared_merit"<br>] |
| `dialog` | [<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "홍보 영상 조회수는 높은데 행사 신청은 기대보다 적어요.",<br>    "de": "Das Werbevideo hat viele Aufrufe, aber weniger Anmeldungen als erwartet.",<br>    "en": "The promo video has many views, but fewer registrations than expected."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "minsu",<br>    "ko": "처음 보는 사람에게는 프로그램 설명이 너무 팬 중심적이었어요.",<br>    "de": "Für Neulinge war die Programmbeschreibung zu stark auf Fans zugeschnitten.",<br>    "en": "For newcomers, the program description was too fan-centered."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "음악뿐 아니라 음식과 드라마도 함께 소개한다는 점에서 입구를 넓힐 수 있어요.",<br>    "de": "Wir können den Einstieg insofern verbreitern, als wir neben Musik auch Essen und Serien vorstellen.",<br>    "en": "We can widen the entry point in that we introduce food and dramas as well as music."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "minsu",<br>    "ko": "초보자를 위한 자막 읽기와 쉬운 후렴 배우기도 넣을까요?",<br>    "de": "Sollen wir Untertitel-Lesen und einen einfachen Refrain für Einsteiger anbieten?",<br>    "en": "Should we add subtitle reading and an easy chorus for beginners?"<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "좋아요. 다만 통역과 진행을 팬 자원봉사에만 맡기면 부담이 커져요.",<br>    "de": "Gut. Aber wenn Übersetzung und Moderation nur bei Fan-Freiwilligen liegen, wird die Belastung zu groß.",<br>    "en": "Good. But if translation and hosting depend only on fan volunteers, the burden becomes too heavy."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "minsu",<br>    "ko": "예산에서 전문 통역 두 시간과 자원봉사 교대 시간을 확보해요.",<br>    "de": "Dann planen wir zwei Stunden professionelle Sprachmittlung und Schichten für Freiwillige ein.",<br>    "en": "Then let's budget for two hours of professional interpretation and volunteer shifts."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "홍보 문구도 ‘진짜 팬만’ 대신 ‘처음이어도 환영’으로 바꾸죠.",<br>    "de": "Ersetzen wir im Werbetext ‚nur echte Fans‘ durch ‚auch beim ersten Mal willkommen‘.",<br>    "en": "Let's replace 'real fans only' with 'first-timers welcome' in the promo copy."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "minsu",<br>    "ko": "그러면 유행을 따라오는 사람도 오래된 팬도 자기 자리를 찾겠네요.",<br>    "de": "Dann finden sowohl Neugierige als auch langjährige Fans ihren Platz.",<br>    "en": "Then both curious newcomers and long-time fans can find a place."<br>  }<br>] |

| `sidekick` | jieun |
| `xpReward` | 180 |
| `title` | {<br>  "ko": "‘감당 가능한 집’의 기준을 묻다",<br>  "de": "Was heißt ‚bezahlbares Wohnen‘?",<br>  "en": "What counts as affordable housing?"<br>} |
| `intro` | {<br>  "ko": "공청회 자료의 ‘월세 안정’이라는 표현이 누구의 어떤 비용을 가리는지 짚고, 감당 가능성의 정의와 지표를 다시 씁니다.",<br>  "de": "In einer Anhörung wird geprüft, wessen Kosten die Formulierung ‚stabile Mieten‘ verdeckt; Definition und Kennzahlen von Bezahlbarkeit werden neu gefasst.",<br>  "en": "At a public hearing, you expose whose costs are hidden by the phrase 'stable rents' and rewrite the definition and measures of affordability."<br>} |
| `grammarIds` | [<br>  "grammar_c2_defined_as"<br>] |
| `dialog` | [<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "보고서는 평균 월세가 안정됐다고 결론짓지만 신규 계약 호가는 별도입니다.",<br>    "de": "Der Bericht erklärt die Durchschnittsmiete für stabil, behandelt Angebotsmieten neuer Verträge aber getrennt.",<br>    "en": "The report concludes that average rent is stable, but asking rents for new leases are a separate matter."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "‘안정’이라는 말이 기존 계약자의 평균을 전체 시장처럼 보이게 하는군요.",<br>    "de": "Das Wort ‚stabil‘ lässt den Durchschnitt bestehender Verträge wie den gesamten Markt erscheinen.",<br>    "en": "The word 'stable' makes the average for existing tenants look like the whole market."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "감당 가능한 주거라 함은 총주거비가 소득과 기본생활을 압박하지 않는 상태를 뜻해야 합니다.",<br>    "de": "Bezahlbares Wohnen sollte als Zustand definiert werden, in dem die gesamten Wohnkosten Einkommen und Grundbedarf nicht verdrängen.",<br>    "en": "Affordable housing should mean a condition in which total housing costs do not crowd out income needed for basic life."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "그 정의라면 월세뿐 아니라 에너지 비용과 퇴거 위험도 들어갑니다.",<br>    "de": "Dann gehören neben Miete auch Energiekosten und Verdrängungsrisiko in die Definition.",<br>    "en": "That definition includes energy costs and displacement risk as well as rent."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "맞습니다. 평균 하나가 아니라 소득 구간별 부담의 분포를 공개해야 합니다.",<br>    "de": "Genau. Statt nur eines Mittels muss die Belastungsverteilung nach Einkommensgruppen veröffentlicht werden.",<br>    "en": "Exactly. We need the distribution of burden by income group, not a single average."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "정책 문구도 ‘가격 안정’에서 ‘부담과 퇴거 위험 완화’로 바꾸겠습니다.",<br>    "de": "Ich ändere die Formulierung von ‚Preisstabilität‘ zu ‚Entlastung und geringeres Verdrängungsrisiko‘.",<br>    "en": "I'll change the policy wording from 'price stability' to 'lower burden and displacement risk.'"<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "다만 어떤 기준선을 썼는지와 지역 차이는 각주가 아니라 본문에 넣어야 합니다.",<br>    "de": "Schwelle und regionale Unterschiede gehören jedoch in den Haupttext, nicht in eine Fußnote.",<br>    "en": "But the threshold used and regional differences belong in the main text, not a footnote."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "그러면 ‘감당 가능’이라는 결론이 측정 방식과 함께 읽히겠네요.",<br>    "de": "Dann wird das Urteil ‚bezahlbar‘ zusammen mit seiner Messweise lesbar.",<br>    "en": "Then the conclusion 'affordable' can be read together with how it was measured."<br>  }<br>] |

| `title` | {<br>  "ko": "취업률 하나가 통합을 대신할 때",<br>  "de": "Wenn Beschäftigung für Integration stehen soll",<br>  "en": "When employment is made to stand for integration"<br>} |
| `intro` | {<br>  "ko": "이민·저출산 특집 기사에서 취업률 하나를 ‘통합 성공’으로 부르는 프레임을 분석하고, 주체·권리·지속성을 포함하도록 문장을 고칩니다.",<br>  "de": "In einem Dossier zu Migration und Demografie wird das Framing von Beschäftigung als ‚gelungene Integration‘ analysiert und um Handlungsmacht, Rechte und Dauer ergänzt.",<br>  "en": "Editing a feature on migration and demographic change, you challenge employment as the sole marker of 'successful integration' and add agency, rights and durability."<br>} |
| `grammarIds` | [<br>  "grammar_c2_nothing_more_than"<br>] |
| `dialog` | [<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "초안은 취업률이 오르면 통합이 성공한 것이라고 단정합니다.",<br>    "de": "Der Entwurf setzt eine höhere Beschäftigungsquote mit gelungener Integration gleich.",<br>    "en": "The draft equates a higher employment rate with successful integration."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "인력 부족을 메웠다는 국가의 관점만 남고 당사자의 삶은 보이지 않네요.",<br>    "de": "Damit bleibt nur die staatliche Perspektive auf Personallücken; das Leben der Betroffenen verschwindet.",<br>    "en": "That leaves only the state's view of filling labor gaps; the lives of the people concerned disappear."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "취업률 하나는 노동시장 편입의 일부 지표에 불과합니다.",<br>    "de": "Die Beschäftigungsquote ist nichts weiter als ein Teilindikator der Arbeitsmarktteilhabe.",<br>    "en": "Employment rate is nothing more than one partial measure of labor-market participation."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "자격에 맞는 일인지, 체류 안정성과 지역 참여가 있는지도 물어야 합니다.",<br>    "de": "Wir müssen auch nach passender Arbeit, Aufenthaltssicherheit und lokaler Teilhabe fragen.",<br>    "en": "We also need to ask about job fit, stability of residence and local participation."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "기사의 주어도 ‘정부가 배치했다’보다 사람들이 어디를 선택했는지 드러내죠.",<br>    "de": "Auch das Subjekt sollte zeigen, was Menschen wählten, statt nur, wohin die Regierung sie vermittelte.",<br>    "en": "The sentence subject should show what people chose, not only where the government placed them."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "난민, 유학생, 노동 이주자를 한 집단으로 묶은 표도 나눠야겠어요.",<br>    "de": "Auch die Tabelle muss Geflüchtete, Studierende und Arbeitsmigranten getrennt ausweisen.",<br>    "en": "We should also separate refugees, students and labor migrants in the table."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "각 범주의 법적 조건이 다른 만큼 비교 가능성과 한계를 명시합시다.",<br>    "de": "Da die rechtlichen Bedingungen verschieden sind, nennen wir Vergleichbarkeit und Grenzen ausdrücklich.",<br>    "en": "Since the legal conditions differ, let's state comparability and limits explicitly."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "jieun",<br>    "ko": "그렇게 고치면 사람을 결핍을 채우는 숫자로만 쓰지 않게 됩니다.",<br>    "de": "So werden Menschen nicht nur als Zahlen zum Füllen eines Mangels dargestellt.",<br>    "en": "That revision stops us from portraying people merely as numbers filling a shortage."<br>  }<br>] |

| `intro` | {<br>  "ko": "국제 문화 포럼에서 ‘진짜 팬’과 ‘한국적 진정성’이라는 표현이 만드는 경계를 분석하고, 상호 번역과 창작자 권리를 전제로 논의를 다시 엽니다.",<br>  "de": "Auf einem internationalen Kulturforum werden die Grenzen von ‚echten Fans‘ und ‚koreanischer Authentizität‘ analysiert und unter der Voraussetzung wechselseitiger Übersetzung neu verhandelt.",<br>  "en": "At an international culture forum, you examine the boundaries created by 'real fans' and 'Korean authenticity,' then reopen the debate on the premise of mutual translation and creator rights."<br>} |
| `grammarIds` | [<br>  "grammar_c2_on_the_premise"<br>] |
| `dialog` | [<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "발제문은 해외 재해석이 늘수록 K-컬처의 진정성이 약해진다고 봅니다.",<br>    "de": "Der Vortrag behauptet, internationale Neuinterpretation schwäche die Authentizität der K-Kultur.",<br>    "en": "The paper argues that international reinterpretation weakens the authenticity of K-culture."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "minsu",<br>    "ko": "그 말은 원본을 지킬 권한이 누구에게 있는지 이미 정해 둔 셈입니다.",<br>    "de": "Damit wird bereits vorausgesetzt, wer das Recht hat, das Original zu bewahren.",<br>    "en": "That wording already assumes who has the authority to preserve the original."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "상호 번역을 전제로 한다면 현지 재해석도 일방적 소비가 아니라 응답이 될 수 있습니다.",<br>    "de": "Unter der Voraussetzung wechselseitiger Übersetzung kann lokale Neuinterpretation eine Antwort statt einseitigen Konsums sein.",<br>    "en": "On the premise of mutual translation, local reinterpretation can be a response rather than one-way consumption."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "minsu",<br>    "ko": "다만 플랫폼이 잘 팔리는 형태만 밀면 그 응답도 특정 틀로 좁아집니다.",<br>    "de": "Wenn Plattformen nur gut verkäufliche Formen fördern, wird auch diese Antwort in ein Raster gedrängt.",<br>    "en": "But when platforms promote only marketable forms, that response is narrowed into a particular mold."<br>  },<br>  {<br>    "speaker": "user",<br>    "ko": "그러니 진정성 논쟁과 별개로 추천 translators and platforms visible."<br>  }<br>] |

| `safeAlternativeQuestions` | [<br>  {<br>    "turnKind": "question",<br>    "ko": "소득별 부담 분포는 공개했어요?",<br>    "de": "Wurde die Belastung nach Einkommen veröffentlicht?",<br>    "en": "Did it publish burden by income?"<br>  }<br>] |
| `followUp` | {<br>  "turnKind": "reaction",<br>  "ko": "그렇다면 ‘안정’의 범위를 본문에서 한정해야겠네요.",<br>  "de": "Dann muss der Geltungsbereich von ‚stabil‘ im Text begrenzt werden.",<br>  "en": "Then the scope of 'stable' needs to be bounded in the main text."<br>} |
